# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:


/usr/local/bin/atb-client
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Participant measurement and submission client; authority stays with the broker."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys


def parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='atb-client',description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--request',type=Path,help='Read a complete request from a JSON file')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command')
    for name in ('help','status','validate'):
        sub.add_parser(name)
    for name in ('estimate','measure'):
        s=sub.add_parser(name)
        if name=='measure':s.add_argument('--id',dest='experiment_id',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--analysis',required=True);s.add_argument('--pvt',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--cl',type=float,help='Load capacitance in farads')
        s.add_argument('--seed',type=int)
    sub.add_parser('submit').add_argument('--rationale',required=True)
    return p


def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,address):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.connect(str(address))
        sock.sendall((json.dumps(request,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
        with sock.makefile('r') as stream:
            reply=json.loads(stream.readline())
    if not isinstance(reply,dict):raise ValueError('Broker reply must be a JSON object')
    return reply


def main(argv=None,workspace=Path('/workspace'),address=Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')):
    args=parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:reply=exchange(request_from(args,Path(workspace)),address)
    except (OSError,ValueError,UnicodeError) as exc:
        # Do not include request contents, circuit bytes or provider credentials.
        reply={'ok':False,'error':type(exc).__name__,'reason':'Cannot read the request/design or exchange a valid broker reply.'}
    print(json.dumps(reply,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False))
    return 0 if reply.get('ok') is True else 1


if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import subprocess,json
base='''.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XMP VOUT PG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m=16
RFBT VOUT FB 50000
RFBB FB VSS 50000
XMBIAS PB PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=5 l=1 m=1
RBIAS PB VSS 30000
XMTAIL TAIL PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=10 l=1 m=1
XMPFB ND FB TAIL VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w={pw} l={pl} m={pm}
XMPREF PG VREF TAIL VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w={pw} l={pl} m={pm}
XMND ND ND VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={nw} l={nl} m={nm}
XMNG PG ND VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={nw} l={nl} m={nm}
CCOMP PG VOUT 1p
.ends DUT
'''
configs=[('v8',100,2,2,10,1,1),('v9',20,1,1,100,2,2),('v10',100,2,2,100,2,2)]
for name,pw,pl,pm,nw,nl,nm in configs:
 circuit=base.format(pw=pw,pl=pl,pm=pm,nw=nw,nl=nl,nm=nm)
 open('dut.spice','w').write(circuit);open('/tmp/'+name+'.spice','w').write(circuit)
 open('REQUEST.json','w').write(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':name+'_nom_n_100n','analysis':'nominal','pvt':'N','cl':1e-7}))
 x=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True);j=json.loads(x.stdout)
 open('/tmp/'+name+'_nom_n_100n.json','w').write(x.stdout)
 print(name,'ok',j['ok'])
 for c in j.get('cases',[]):
  m=c.get('measurements',{});k=c['case_id']

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import subprocess,json
base=open('/tmp/v25.spice').read()
for r in [7000,12000,20000]:
 circuit=base.replace('RSD NSD VSS 3000',f'RSD NSD VSS {r}').replace('RSG NSG VSS 3000',f'RSG NSG VSS {r}')
 open('dut.spice','w').write(circuit);open(f'/tmp/v25_d{r}.spice','w').write(circuit)
 print('D',r)
 for pvt in ['N','SLC','FHH']:
  ident=f'v25_d{r}_nom_{pvt}_100n'
  open('REQUEST.json','w').write(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':ident,'analysis':'nominal','pvt':pvt,'cl':1e-7}))
  j=json.loads(subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout)
  open(f'/tmp/{ident}.json','w').write(json.dumps(j))
  print('PVT',pvt,'ok',j['ok'])
  for c in j.get('cases',[]):
   m=c.get('measurements',{});k=c['case_id']
   if k in ('light_heavy','burst','line'):
    e=m.get('envelope',{});print(k,'err',round(max(e.get('droop_v',0),e.get('overshoot_v',0)),4),'rec',[None if w.get('recovery_s') is None else round(w['recovery_s']*1e6,1) for w in m.get('recovery_windows',[])])
   elif k in ('dc_low','dc_high','psrr_high','noise_low','noise_high'):print(k,m.get('vout_v'),m.get('psrr_db',{}).get('1000'),m.get('noise',{}).get('output_noise_rms_v'))
PY"
MODEL

The degeneration measurements are still running across three PVT groups. I’m comparing the same revision in each group so I can keep any PSRR gain only if the DC voltage and load recovery remain within reach.

MODEL

The mirror resistor tied to VIN appears to inject too much supply variation into the bias: larger mirror degeneration improves hot PSRR but barely helps cold. I’m moving that bias resistor to regulated VOUT and raising its mirror ratio. This should hold amplifier current steadier during the VIN sweep while staying inside the overhead limit.

MODEL

Asymmetric mirror resistors create large DC offsets, so I’m keeping them matched. I’m testing a separate fast input pair whose gates are equal in DC and whose feedback gate receives only the changing part of VOUT. The long pair keeps the DC setpoint; the short pair may improve cold load steps without adding low frequency noise.

MODEL

The added amplifier did not improve PSRR enough and pushed the hot, light-load operating point just outside the DC limit. I’ve returned to the better single-amplifier circuit and am tuning a small VIN-to-gate feedforward capacitor, which can reduce supply ripple at the pass transistor without changing its DC bias.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

Defaults and seed requirements:

- nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- dc: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.

- dc_mid: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_high: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.

- mid_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- burst: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- line: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_low: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_high: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- noise_low: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- noise_high: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

import json
from collections import defaultdict
r=defaultdict(lambda:[float('inf'),None])
for cl,suffix in ((1e-7,'100'),(1e-6,'1u')):
 for p in ('N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH'):
  j=json.load(open(f'/tmp/rff4z400_nom{suffix}_{p}.json'))
  for x in j['summary']['spec_table']:
   if x['verdict']"'!='"'PASS':raise Exception((cl,p,x))
   if x.get('direction')=='<=' and isinstance(x.get('measured'),(float,int)) and x.get('limit') and x['limit']>0:
    m=(x['limit']-x['measured'])/x['limit']
    if m<r[x['metric']][0]:r[x['metric']]=[m,(cl,p,x['case_id'],x['measured'],x['limit'])]
print('All 18 full suites pass')
for k,(m,x) in sorted(r.items()):print(k,round(m*100,2),x)
PY"
